## `AFG-9101-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Polarizing-Fiber-Microscope Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Archival Air Micro-Pollutant & Paper Surface pH
- **Intro:** Polarizing-Fiber-Microscope Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Archival Air Micro-Pollutant & Paper Surface pH — Pre-calibration Environmental Baseline Check: Temperature: 20±1°C; Relative Humidity: 50±5% RH; PM2.5: ≤10 µg/m³; PM10: ≤20 µg/m³; TVOC: ≤100 µg/m³
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 20±1°C; Relative Humidity: 50±5% RH; PM2.5: ≤10 µg/m³; PM10: ≤20 µg/m³; TVOC: ≤100 µg/m³
- **Standards:** `ISO 9001:2015`, `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`, `ANSI/NISO Z39.79-2001 Environmental Conditions for Storage of Pa`, `ISO 11799:2015`
